### `src/libs/diff_export.sh` -- `tests/test_diff_export.sh`, `tests/test_diff_dispatch.sh`

Subject: the export orchestrator. `diff_export SANDBOX_DIR OUTPUT_DIR [SESSION_ID]` calls `package_branch` with rename detection off, writes `.export-status` as SUCCESS or FAIL, and on failure writes a timestamped error log holding the callee's captured stderr. `_write_export_error_log OUTPUT_DIR TIMESTAMP [SESSION_ID] [EXIT_CODE] [STDERR_DUMP] [SUMMARY]` writes that log. `wait_git_lockfile SANDBOX_DIR [TIMEOUT_SECS]` polls for `.git/index.lock` to disappear and returns 1 if it persists. Two entrypoint call sites use it: the session exit export and the autosave loop; `scripts/dry_run_capability.sh` calls it directly.

The module is an exception reporter, so its failure paths carry the weight. Two of them do not hold, and both are demonstrated below: a failed internal package is reported as SUCCESS (finding 68), and the stderr capture is written into a directory the callee deletes (finding 69).

Units (9 in `test_diff_export.sh` for this module plus 8 in `test_diff_dispatch.sh`; the file's five `_write_export_status` units belong to `export_status.sh` and its five `test_session_path_*` units are integration tests over `export_path` and `diff_export`):

| Group | Units | Asserts | Bite |
|---|---|---|---|
| `diff_export` failure path | `test_diff_export_failure_writes_export_status`, `_writes_error_log` | FAIL status, the exit code, and the log's existence | proven (E3, E4, E5) |
| artifact writes | `test_diff_export_creates_output`, `_writes_uncommitted_diff`, `_writes_all_changes_diff`, `_writes_patches`, `_writes_changed_files`, `_no_sweep_commit` | the four artifacts land and the tree is left dirty | proven (E6 for the no-renames argument) |
| argument and baseline guards | `test_diff_export_missing_args_fails`, `_missing_session_state` | rc 1 for a missing argument and for a missing baseline | partially pinned (E15 survives) |
| `_write_export_error_log` | `test_export_error_log_creates_file`, `_includes_session_id`, `_contains_error_details` | file, session-id suffix, exit code, run id, stderr | proven (E13), with the SUMMARY line unpinned (E14) |
| `wait_git_lockfile` | `test_wait_git_lockfile_no_lockfile`, `_lockfile_appears_and_disappears`, `_timeout`, `_timeout_message` | rc and message per case | proven (E11, E12), with the poll budget unpinned (E10) |

Bite checks (subject restored after each; every mutation ran the four export suites):

| Mutation | Units failed | Reading |
|---|---|---|
| E1 capture file moved out of `OUTPUT_DIR` | none | **survived** - nothing pins where the capture lives (finding 69) |
| E2 success-path capture cleanup removed | none | **survived** - a stray capture file in a bundle would go unnoticed (finding 69) |
| E3 FAIL status written as SUCCESS | 1 | the FAIL record is pinned |
| E4 failure exit code not propagated | 2 | the rc contract is pinned |
| E5 error-log write removed | 1 | the log's existence is pinned |
| E6 no-renames argument flipped | 1 | the packaging argument is pinned |
| E7 timestamp no longer UTC | none | **survived** - the UTC base is unpinned (row 31) |
| E8 `init_sha` read guard removed | none | **survived** - result-redundant (finding 71) |
| E9 lockfile early return disabled | none | **survived** - result-redundant (finding 70) |
| E10 poll budget cut to one fifth | none | **survived** - the wait semantics are unpinned (finding 70) |
| E11 poll interval shortened | 1 | the release-within-timeout case is pinned |
| E12 persistent lock no longer fails | 1 | the timeout verdict is pinned |
| E13 session id dropped from the log filename | 2 | the filename contract is pinned |
| E14 SUMMARY line dropped from the log | none | **survived** - unpinned (finding 71) |
| E15 `OUTPUT_DIR` argument clause removed | none | **survived** - partial guards unpinned (finding 13) |
| E16 argument-guard return flipped | no-op | the mutation reproduced the original text |

Probes for the two failure-path defects. For finding 68: a sandbox with a base commit and a second commit whose blob object is truncated to zero bytes; `git rev-parse HEAD` still resolves and the preflight tolerates its own `|| true`, so the run proceeds. Under `set -euo pipefail`, the per-commit `git diff` fails with `error: object file ... is empty` and `fatal: unable to read`, the patch lands as 1 byte, `changed-files/` still receives its copy, and the result is `STATUS=SUCCESS`, rc 0, and no error log. For finding 69: `mktemp` inside a directory, then `rm -rf` and `mkdir -p` of that directory, then the failure-path test `[[ -f "$file" ]]` - the file reports absent while the held descriptor keeps accepting writes, so the dump is empty.

### `src/libs/session_env.sh` -- `tests/test_session_env.sh`, `tests/test_checkpoint.sh`

Subject: the host-side prelude, in two phases plus one shared formula. `session_env_common_init PROJECT_NAME PROJECT_DIR SANDBOX_DIR` (phase 1, no session id) resolves the identity triple through `env_resolve_identity`, fails when `ENV_FILE` is absent (with an onboard hint), loads the file with `env_load`, re-asserts the three resolved identity values so a conflicting `.env` cannot shadow them, validates that the project dir is a git repository with at least one commit, derives the harness paths with `dirs_resolve`, and exports `HOST_UID`, `HOST_GID`, and `ENV_FILE`. `session_env_names PROJECT_NAME PROVIDER_NAME SANDBOX_DIR SESSION_ID` (phase 2, needs the session id) derives the sanitised host branch, the image and container names, and the `WORKTREE_DIR` default. `session_id_derive SANDBOX_DIR HOST_HEAD_SHA SESSION_TS` hashes the canonicalised dir, the head SHA, and the timestamp into the 6-character session id.

Two inputs arrive as globals rather than parameters: phase 2 reads `PROJECT_DIR` for the branch (l.94, l.96) and `WORKTREE_DIR` for its default (l.115), so its four-argument signature is a subset of its real inputs (finding 72). The top `Provides:` block also states phase 1's argument order as `<sandbox_dir> <project_name> <project_dir>`, while the signature and the comment directly above the function are `(project_name, project_dir, sandbox_dir)` (row 12).

Units (21 in `test_session_env.sh`; 5 in `test_checkpoint.sh` for `session_id_derive`, plus 2 drift guards in that file that are adjacent rather than subject):

| Group | Units | Asserts | Bite |
|---|---|---|---|
| `.env` parse rules (7) | `test_env_comments_and_blanks_skipped`, `_key_whitespace_stripped_value_trimmed`, `_inline_comment_is_kept_as_value`, `_whitespace_only_key_line_skipped`, `_indented_comment_skipped`, `_invalid_identifier_key_skipped_with_warning`, plus `test_common_init_exports_identity_and_paths` for the load itself | comments, blanks, TAB/CRLF keys, trimmed values, the pinned absence of an inline-comment rule, and the three regressions | proven (V19: replacing the load with `/dev/null` fails all 7) |
| phase 1 failures (3) | `test_env_missing_file_fails_with_onboard_hint`, `test_common_init_rejects_non_git_project`, `_rejects_commitless_repo` | the three refusal paths and their messages | proven (V4, V5) |
| phase 1 exports (1) | `test_common_init_exports_identity_and_paths` | `PROJECT_NAME`, `PROJECT_DIR`, `ENV_FILE`, `HOST_UID`/`HOST_GID`, and a loaded `.env` variable | proven (V15: a hardcoded `HOST_UID` fails it) |
| identity precedence (5) | `test_env_project_name_explicit_arg_beats_env_and_envvar`, `_project_dir_explicit_arg_beats_env`, `_identity_explicit_args_beat_env`, `_resolves_identity_from_env_when_args_empty`, `_ag_sandbox_envvar_beats_env` | explicit beats `AGENT_SANDBOX_*` beats `.env` beats the fallback | proven (V1, V2) |
| `--env` pointer (2) | `test_env_resolves_with_absolute_env_pointer`, `_with_relative_env_pointer` | `ENV_REL` absolute and sandbox-relative, and the `ENV_FILE` that results | read-assessed (exercises `env_resolve.sh`) |
| phase 2 names (4) | `test_names_sanitises_host_branch`, `_detached_head_falls_back_to_short_sha`, `_deterministic_container_and_image_names`, `_worktree_var_defaults_and_overrides` | the sanitise class, the fallback, the four derived names, and the default-with-override read | proven (V6, V7, V8, V9, V18); the sandbox image name is asserted by substring only |
| `session_id_derive` (5) | `test_session_id_returns_6_chars`, `_is_hex`, `_stable_across_calls`, `_sensitive_to_all_factors`, `_converges_across_path_spellings` | length, case, determinism, factor sensitivity, and canonicalisation | proven (V10, V11, V12) |
| drift guards (2, adjacent) | `test_sandbox_id_functions_removed`, `test_no_inline_identity_pipelines_remain` | the removed intermediate and the absence of inline re-derivation | read-assessed |

Bite checks (subject restored after each; every mutation ran both suites, 26 units; baseline 21 + 7):

| Mutation | Units failed | Reading |
|---|---|---|
| V1 drop the `PROJECT_NAME` re-assert | 3 | proven - flag-wins at the name level is pinned |
| V2 drop the `PROJECT_DIR` re-assert | 2 | proven |
| V3 drop the `SANDBOX_DIR` re-assert | none | **survived** - no fixture gives `.env` a conflicting `SANDBOX_DIR` (finding 74) |

Fourteen mutations are proven and six survive. Two of the survivors are behaviour that should be pinned (findings 74 and 75); one is a genuine redundancy (V14); one is a partial-guard gap that belongs to row 13 (V17); and two are exports whose loss the container names do not feel, because those are built from the locals (V16, V20).

The suite's centre of gravity is elsewhere: seven of the 21 units here test `env_load`, a function this file only calls, and three of the seven duplicate direct units in `test_env.sh` (finding 73). That placement carried the parser's regressions until now, and it left the parser's own file as the weaker suite.

### `src/libs/resume_list.sh` -- no test file (no suite references the lib)

Subject: the resume table. `build_inventory` parses the session registry into `RESUME_INVENTORY` (one record per line, eight fields, newest first by session timestamp, dry-run sessions skipped); `_no_sessions` prints the empty-inventory guidance and returns 1; `_resume_truncate_branch` caps a branch name at 11 characters plus an ellipsis; `_resume_work_map` fills `_WORK_MAP[sid]` with `<N>c[+u]` from the newest checkpoint directory under `.workspace/session-diffs` (the autosave directory first, else the newest session export whose name ends in `-<sid>`); `_resume_state_map` fills `_STATE_MAP[sid]` from one `docker ps -a` call; `_resume_state_cell` merges the activity log's last event with the docker verb; `_resume_render_rows` prints the paged table (list mode) or fills `PICKER` (interactive mode).

This is the read-through's largest coverage gap. The lib has no unit at all, so mutation bites have nothing to bite: the behaviour below was established by direct probes instead of mutations. The probe harness stubs the read dependencies (`enumerate_records`, `session_is_dry_run`, `session_stale`, `session_log_read`, `record_label`, `project_current_sha`, `project_branch_age`, `relative_time_compact`) and calls the real functions, including the real `_resume_state_map` against a stub `docker`.

Units: none. No BDD write-back is possible, because there is no test function to annotate. Every behaviour below is **probe-verified**.

| Probe | Given / When / Then | Verdict |
|---|---|---|
| 1 branch truncation | names of 10, 11, 12, and 20 characters | 10 and 11 pass through; 12 and 20 become 11 characters plus an ellipsis |
| 2 inventory build | three registry records, one of them a dry-run session | two entries, eight fields each, newest timestamp first, the dry-run session skipped |
| 3 work map, autosave | autosave directory with two patch files and a zero-byte `uncommitted.diff` | `2c` - the `-s` gate ignores an empty diff |
| 4 work map, uncommitted | one patch file and a non-empty `uncommitted.diff` | `1c+u` |
| 5 work map, source preference | an autosave directory and a three-patch session export 14 hours newer | `1c` from the autosave directory; `3c` after removing it |
| 6 work map, neither | a session with no checkpoint directory | `--` |
| 7 docker map | a stub `docker ps -a` reporting `running` and `exited` | `running` and `stopped` |
| 8 state cell, live container | log says started, docker says running | `running`, with no age - the defect in finding 80 |
| 9 state cell, stopped container | log says stopped, docker says stopped | `stopped 5m ago` - the verbs agree here, so the age survives |
| 10 state cell, no docker | log says started, empty docker map | `started 5m ago` |
| 11 state cell, later stop | log has stop after start, docker says running | `running` |
| 12 state cell, no events | no log events at all | `-` |
| 13 empty-inventory message | `PROVIDER_FILTER` empty, then set | rc 1, with the sandbox-path message in one case and the provider message in the other |
| 14 list render | page size 2, two entries | header plus two rows on stdout; zero lines on stderr, though the docstring says stderr |
| 15 interactive render | same inventory | two `PICKER` entries, each a value followed by its rendered row; `_RESUME_HEADER` filled |
| 16 empty inventory | empty `RESUME_INVENTORY` | header only, zero picker entries |
| 17 docker absent | stub docker returning 127, and an empty registry | empty `_STATE_MAP`; zero inventory entries |

Three defects follow from the probes: the age disappears for every live session (finding 80), the stale marker breaks the alignment (finding 81), and the docstrings disagree with the code in four places (row 12). The file's display conventions are otherwise sound and consistent with the draft bundle table, which is the same AGE-as-wall-clock, STATE-as-commit-distance split.

### `src/libs/package_branch.sh` -- `tests/test_package_branch.sh` (20 units) and `tests/test_diff_rename.sh` (3)

Subject: the bundle dispatcher, 429 lines and five functions. `package_commits SANDBOX_DIR OUT NO_RENAMES SINCE_SHA` writes one numbered patch per commit since the baseline, with a sibling `.msg` file holding the full message, and overwrites its target each run. `_package_preflight_check SANDBOX_DIR INIT_SHA` warns, advisory and always rc 0, when the working tree modifies a file that appears in the committed diff, or when a file's content at the baseline equals its working-tree content. `package_branch_baseline SANDBOX_DIR [EXPLICIT]` prints the explicit baseline after validating it resolves to a commit, else the merge-base of the recorded `init_sha` and `HEAD`. `package_branch SANDBOX_DIR OUTPUT_DIR [NO_RENAMES] [BASELINE]` resolves the baseline, refuses a repository whose state it cannot read, wipes and recreates the output directory, runs the four writers, stamps `.export-status`, and prints the host draft hint. The direct-mode block parses flags, requires `--to` and `--bundle-summary`, resolves the output path through `export_path`, and calls the dispatcher.

This file was read once already, during the `diff.sh` pass, for the inline per-commit diff writer (findings 59, 64, and 65) and the `rm -rf` that destroys the caller's stderr capture (finding 69). This pass covers the rest of the module.

Units (23: 20 in `test_package_branch.sh`, 3 in `test_diff_rename.sh`; the export suites carry integration units over the same code path):

| Group | Units | Asserts | Bite |
|---|---|---|---|
| artefact layout (5) | `test_dispatcher_creates_all_artefacts`, `_diffs_numbered`, `_overwrites_output`, `_includes_untracked_in_changed_files`, `_no_commits` | the six artefact types present, numbering, patch-count replacement, untracked copies, the empty range | proven (B11 for the status file); the `.msg` siblings are absent from every list (finding 82) |
| patch fidelity (4) | `test_dispatcher_diff_is_applicable`, `_contains_content`, `_strips_text_index_keeps_binary_index`, `_binary_patch_applies_to_fresh_repo` | the patch applies, carries its body, keeps the binary index line, and round-trips a binary | proven (B8, B9) |
| argument and state guards (4) | `test_dispatcher_missing_args`, `_missing_session_state`, `_refuses_unreadable_repository`, `test_baseline_explicit_override` | rc non-zero for both missing cases, refusal for a corrupt index, and the explicit baseline honoured | proven (B3, B1) |
| export metadata (1) | `test_dispatcher_export_status_contents` | STATUS, TIMESTAMP, INIT_SHA, and the absence of HEAD | proven (B11, three units) |
| baseline resolution (1) | `test_baseline_defaults_to_branch_point` | the branch-point fallback when the recorded `init_sha` is orphaned | proven (B1) |
| preflight (5) | `test_preflight_bypass_returns_before_any_git`, `_clean_tree_is_silent`, `_flags_uncommitted_modifications`, `_flags_cancelled_out_modification`, `_skips_deleted_files_without_warning` | the bypass, the silent clean tree, both advisories, and the deleted-file skip | proven (B5); see row 8 for the two weak units |
| rename detection (3) | `test_package_branch_no_renames_true`, `_default_detects_rename`, `test_diff_export_uses_no_renames_by_default` | rename detection off, on, and the export default | proven (via B8's neighbourhood) |

Bite checks (subject restored after each; every mutation ran the three suites, 37 units; baseline 20 + 3 + 14):

| Mutation | Units failed | Reading |
|---|---|---|
| B1 drop the merge-base (baseline = init_sha) | 1 | proven |
| B2 drop the explicit-baseline validation | none | **survived** - the guard is untested (finding 85a) |
| B3 drop the refuse-state guard | 1 | proven |
| B4 drop the preflight bypass | none | **survived** - its unit cannot tell the short-circuit from a git run that yields nothing (row 8) |
| B5 drop the cancelled-out advisory | 1 | proven |
| B6 drop the deleted-file skip | none | **survived** - the skip is result-redundant (row 8) |
| B7 drop the dispatcher's `rm -rf` | none | **survived** - `changed-files/` keeps stale copies (finding 83) |
| B8 drop `--binary` from the per-commit diff | 2 | proven |
| B9 bypass `strip_index_lines` | 1 | proven |
| B10 drop the trailing-newline append | none | **survived** - extends row 61 |
| B11 drop the `.export-status` write | 3 | proven |

| B13 drop `BRANCH_FROM` from the hint | none | **survived** - extends row 37 |
| B14 drop the preflight call | none | **survived** - the wiring is untested (finding 85b) |
| B15 drop the subject sanitisation | none | **survived** - the filename shape is untested (finding 85c) |
| B16 drop the `.msg` write | none | **survived** - a host-consumed artefact has no unit (finding 82) |
| B17 all-changes writer without a baseline | none | **survived** - confirms row 66's dead default |
| B18 drop the inner `rm` in `package_commits` | none | **survived** - result-redundant (finding 85d) |

Six mutations are proven and twelve survive. Of the survivors, three are unpinned behaviours that matter (the `.msg` files, the `changed-files/` staleness, the preflight wiring), four extend rows already recorded, and one confirms a recorded dependency claim.

The guard probe is worth stating exactly, because it distinguishes the two halves of the refuse-state comment: with a corrupt index, `git status --porcelain` returns 128 and `package_branch` refuses as the comment promises; with a truncated blob (an unreadable object store), the same command returns 0, `git rev-parse HEAD` returns 0, and the per-commit `git diff` returns 128 - so the run proceeds and, per finding 68's probe, still stamps STATUS=SUCCESS over a one-byte patch.
